/home/vgpnk/Projects/shokunin/kotodama-shokunin/corpus/knowledge/linguistics/language-contact.md:27:Worked contrast: English after 1066 kept Germanic grammar while flooding with French/Latin lexicon (borrowing). Irish English and Indian English keep few source-language words but show substrate syntax/idiom — Irish "after-perfect" (*I'm after doing it*), Indian English focus particle *only* and invariant tag *isn't it* (shift). Same colonizer, opposite signatures, because the sociolinguistic event differed. Cf. Dawkins (1916) for the extreme shift case: some Asia Minor Greek varieties borrowed Turkish **inflectional verbal morphology** — the textbook ceiling of contact depth.
/home/vgpnk/Projects/shokunin/kotodama-shokunin/corpus/knowledge/myth-symbol/flood-traditions.md:40:- Vocabulary does cosmological work: the "fountains of the great deep" burst open — *tehom*, cognate with Akkadian *tiâmtu* (Tiamat; direct derivation debated) — and a wind (*ruach*) sweeps the waters, the same vocabulary as Genesis 1. The flood is a controlled un-creation: the cosmos reverts to its day-2 state and is re-sorted with "be fruitful and multiply" re-issued. Cf. Psalm 104:5–9, where the boundary-set waters are the *standing* defeat of chaos; the flood is chaos granted one episode of parole. Cf. also Enūma Eliš VI, where Marduk's bow is elevated as the constellation "the Bow" (Dalley 1989) — bow-as-sign has a Mesopotamian precedent.

Alternative derivation read at source: C. M. Watson, *British Weights and Measures as Described in the Laws of England from Anglo-Saxon Times* (1910), pp. 82–83, from the archive.org full text — "troy" probably < OE dialect *troi* "a balance", *tron* another form and also "market, or place of weighing", keyed to the Public Record Commissioners' rendering of *troni ponderacionem* in the Assize of Weights and Measures. Watson's sole lexical witness was checked directly: Wright, *English Dialect Dictionary*, vol. 6 s.v. **TROY** (archive.org text), "A steelyard; a pair of scales; gen. used in pl. Watson (1910) and Wright (1898) added to `- sources:` and the OED entry point named there.

/home/vgpnk/Projects/shokunin/kumitate-shokunin/corpus/knowledge/remaining-scripts-census.md:620:The shared credential-shape screen used by both the native-trial launcher and the handoff collector, deliberately byte-exact to the judge's `detail_contains_credential` mirror. `containsCredentialShape` (`scripts/ufo-credential-screen.mjs:18`) applies an ASCII-only fold and matches three literal needles in `SECRET_LITERAL_NEEDLES` (`:14`) plus provider token prefixes in `SECRET_TOKEN_NEEDLES` (`:15`) only when at least `SECRET_TOKEN_MIN = 12` token characters follow (`:16`) — so `sk-42` never trips. The evidence-writing side is wider: `containsSensitiveShape` (`:62`) adds `SENSITIVE_LITERAL_RE` (`:51`, catching the compact `eyJ…` JWT shape and `PRIVATE KEY`) and re-scans text stripped by `FORMAT_CHARS_RE` (`:61`, applied at `:65-68`), because invisible Cf characters can split a shape without changing what a reader copies out. This regex does not strip Mn or Zs. The widening lives only on this side — stripping in the judge mirror would break parity, so callers layer their own shapes on top rather than editing the needle sets. A value either side would redact must never reach `launch.json`, `rejection.txt`, `handoff.json` or an `effectiveLaunch` host field.
